- **halopsa**: Every HaloPSA, HaloITSM and HaloCRM feature, plus a local SQLite store and cross-entity views the API can't return. Trigger phrases: `triage my Halo queue`, `check SLA breaches in HaloPSA`, `who is overloaded in Halo`, `client card for Acme in Halo`, `Halo contract burn-down`, `what changed in Halo since this morning`, `find time gaps in my Halo timesheet`, `use halopsa`, `run halopsa`. ([Servosity/msp-skills](https://github.com/Servosity/msp-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/halopsa) · ★83)
- **HaloPSA Agents**: HaloPSA agents (technicians) and teams as a read-only MCP surface: listing technicians, retrieving agent detail, listing team structures, and the inactive-agent filter. Agent IDs resolved here are what ticket assignment needs. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/halopsa/halopsa/skills/agents) · ★55)
- **HaloPSA API Patterns**: HaloPSA REST API fundamentals: OAuth 2.0 client-credentials authentication, authorization vs. resource server URLs, the tenant query parameter, filtering and pagination conventions, array-wrapped POST bodies, rate-limit behavior, scopes, and error codes. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/halopsa/halopsa/skills/api-patterns) · ★55)
- **HaloPSA Assets**: HaloPSA asset/CMDB data model: asset (configuration item) fields, device types and statuses, links to clients, sites, users, tickets, and contracts, plus parent-child asset relationships. Covers the Asset API surface, hardware lifecycle workflows, RMM auto-sync and device matching, and validation errors. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/halopsa/halopsa/skills/assets) · ★55)
- **HaloPSA Clients**: HaloPSA CRM data model: client records and their billing/contact fields, sites (locations), contacts (Users), client classification, and parent-child client hierarchy. Covers the Client/Site/Users API surface, onboarding and deactivation workflows, duplicate prevention, and common validation errors. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/halopsa/halopsa/skills/clients) · ★55)
- **HaloPSA Contracts**: HaloPSA contract management: contract types (recurring, prepaid hours, ad-hoc, project, warranty), statuses, billing and coverage fields, recurring invoice items, prepaid hour balances and deduction, SLA association, renewal and billing-reconciliation workflows. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/halopsa/halopsa/skills/contracts) · ★55)
- **HaloPSA Invoices**: HaloPSA invoices as a read-only MCP surface: listing by client or date range, filtering by payment and send status, retrieving line-item detail on a single invoice, and the reporting and reconciliation workflows built on them. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/halopsa/halopsa/skills/invoices) · ★55)
- **HaloPSA Tickets**: HaloPSA service desk tickets: ticket fields, statuses, priorities and ticket types, actions (notes and time entries), attachments, SLA behaviour, and the creation and status-transition workflows with their validation rules. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/halopsa/halopsa/skills/tickets) · ★55)
- **halos-ai-shell**: Use when working on SAINT agent mode, the OS action registry, trust levels, visual multi-step plans, parallel task tiling, background (focus-free) automation, Claude Code headless integration, AIDE integration, or camera presence. ([uberdiz/Halo](https://github.com/uberdiz/Halo/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/halos-ai-shell) · ★1)
- **halos-desktop-design**: Use when working on HalOS Settings, HalOS Files (search), the icon theme, the living wallpaper/SAINT visualizer, animations, or any HalOS-made UI. ([uberdiz/Halo](https://github.com/uberdiz/Halo/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/halos-desktop-design) · ★1)
- **halp**: Use this skill when the user wants to Find help for a CLI command or Print version. ([javimosch/supercli](https://github.com/javimosch/supercli/tree/HEAD/plugins/halp/skills/quickstart) · ★58)
- **halpern-argument-analysis**: Use this skill when the user wants guided Halpern Argument Analysis applied to real work. It teaches the source-grounded method, halts for observable user work, evaluates each gate, and produces a structured handoff. ([cerredz/Vidbyte-Skills](https://github.com/cerredz/Vidbyte-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/halpern-argument-analysis))
- **halt**: Use this skill when the user wants to use halt, cli tool: halt. ([javimosch/supercli](https://github.com/javimosch/supercli/tree/HEAD/plugins/halt/skills/quickstart) · ★58)
- **halt**: Pause the current Chariot mission. Defaults to graceful (5s grace, then SIGKILL). Use --force for immediate SIGKILL. ([dc143cDev/claude-chariot](https://github.com/dc143cDev/claude-chariot/tree/HEAD/skills/halt))
- **halt**: load when dima types /clio:halt or says «halt», the one word that starts it. A softer way of ending («i'm done», «let's wrap») is no halt, and a bare «stop» is a hold: ask in one line. ([dvakatsiienko/frame](https://github.com/dvakatsiienko/frame/tree/HEAD/_clio/plugin-clio/skills/halt) · 2 copies)
- **halt**: 用户要求任务到此终止时用它——停下手上的一切，不再做事、不回复、不输出任何内容，等用户直接输入再醒。 ([faliye/singlefs](https://github.com/faliye/singlefs/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/halt) · 5 copies)
- **halt**: HALT（HTMX+Atomic+Lit+Templ）フロントエンドアーキテクチャを仕様書に追加する ([ousiass/dotfiles](https://github.com/ousiass/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/omp/skills/halt) · 2 copies)
- **halt**: HALT（HTMX+Atomic+Lit+Templ）フロントエンドアーキテクチャを仕様書に追加する。ユーザーが新規/既存プロジェクトに HALT アーキテクチャの設計セクションを追加したいとき、フロントエンド技術スタックを HALT に統一したいとき、HALT 仕様書を新規作成したいときに使う。 ([ousiass/dotfiles](https://github.com/ousiass/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/halt))
- **halt-as-deliverable**: When a pre-registration, pre-flight or sanity gate refuses to produce your deliverable, the catch is often worth more than the deliverable. Use when a discipline you built caught your own work. ([MrBinnacle/skills](https://github.com/MrBinnacle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/halt-as-deliverable) · ★1)
- **halt-authority**: hold the single stop-control finding for the agent. ([deagy/cadre](https://github.com/deagy/cadre/tree/HEAD/hermes-plugins/skills/cadre/review/halt-authority) · ★1)

- **halting-on-ambiguity**: Use when two code paths do the same job under different names, the same setting is declared in two config surfaces, a field or table or responsibility has no declared owner, authoritative prose carries soft modal hedges with no enforcement path (the policy `rule-softness` in `ambiguity-policies.contract.json` enumerates the exact tokens), runtime behavior contradicts documented behavior, a test asserts one thing while production does another, a domain owner is unclear, a generic folder (`shared/`, `common/`, `utils/`, `lib/`, `core/`, `misc/`) is about to absorb new logic with no charter, or… ([craftogrammer/firstlaw](https://github.com/craftogrammer/firstlaw/tree/HEAD/.law/skills/halting-on-ambiguity))

- **halucatch**: Evaluates the reliability of AI Skill execution. Assesses whether a Skill's output is trustworthy, reproducible, and withstands business scrutiny when executed by an AI agent. Covers four dimensions: data pipeline integrity, code risk, business logic ambiguity, and interpretation guardrails. Used for auditing AI skills, detecting hallucinations or unreliable outputs, verifying reproducibility, and reviewing safety before deployment. Requires a specific skill directory path as input. ([CoderMoray/HaluCatch](https://github.com/CoderMoray/HaluCatch/tree/HEAD/halucatch) · ★1)
- **halusinasyon-engelleyici**: Halüsinasyona müsait görevin çıktısını doğrulama zinciriyle üretir; her iddia DESTEKLİ/DAYANAKSIZ/ÇELİŞİR etiketli, önce H derecesi hesaplanır (derece aracı). Tetik; emirde `halusinasyon_derecesi ≥ eşik` (7 / pahalı-geri-alma 4) ya da çıktıda atıf/künye/DOI/URL/kaynakça, "halüsinasyon freniyle üret", "doğrulama zinciriyle yaz", olgusal/sayısal/atıf-yoğun raporda. kullanılmaz; H&lt;4 rutin işte, sohbette, test/yürütme kapısı zaten doğruluyorsa, skill'i/formülü değiştirmek için. argument-hint: "&lt;görev + kaynaklar | --derece bayrakları&gt; ([salihsahinin/sistem](https://github.com/salihsahinin/sistem/tree/HEAD/jarvis/.claude/skills/halusinasyon-engelleyici))
- **halvard**: Run the shared application-development flow from requirements and branch creation through verification and independent review. Use for starting, resuming, or finishing implementation tasks. ([ghulando/halvard](https://github.com/ghulando/halvard/tree/HEAD/skills/halvard) · ★4)
- **halvard-benchmark**: Vet a performance number before you report or act on it, by naming its limiter and checking tuning, physical limits, errors, repeatability, relevance and whether the work ran. Use when you run a benchmark, set a performance baseline, or report a speedup or regression you measured. ([ghulando/halvard](https://github.com/ghulando/halvard/tree/HEAD/skills/halvard-benchmark) · ★4)
- **halvard-bootstrap**: Configure a repository's stack rules and skills from shared stack records, asking only what a record leaves open. Use for a new or empty repository, or to add stack skills to an existing one. ([ghulando/halvard](https://github.com/ghulando/halvard/tree/HEAD/skills/halvard-bootstrap) · ★4)
- **halvard-correct**: Find the mistakes agents keep repeating in a repository and make each one impossible to repeat, preferring structure, then types, then a lint that names the fix, then a test, with docs last. Use only when the user asks for it, including for an actionable halvard-retro finding; ordinary corrections do not trigger this skill. ([ghulando/halvard](https://github.com/ghulando/halvard/tree/HEAD/skills/halvard-correct) · ★4)
- **halvard-debug**: Reproduce and diagnose an application bug or performance regression before fixing its cause. Use for failures, incorrect behavior or unexplained slowness. ([ghulando/halvard](https://github.com/ghulando/halvard/tree/HEAD/skills/halvard-debug) · ★4)
- **halvard-design**: Choose interfaces, data shapes and module ownership before consequential application changes. Use when implementation would lock in an unclear architecture. ([ghulando/halvard](https://github.com/ghulando/halvard/tree/HEAD/skills/halvard-design) · ★4)
- **halvard-handoff**: Preserve portable task context and safely resume it in another harness. Use at session boundaries or when continuing existing work. ([ghulando/halvard](https://github.com/ghulando/halvard/tree/HEAD/skills/halvard-handoff) · ★4)
- **halvard-implement**: Implement a planned application feature or clear fix in small verified steps. Use once requirements and project conventions are understood. ([ghulando/halvard](https://github.com/ghulando/halvard/tree/HEAD/skills/halvard-implement) · ★4)
- **halvard-retro**: Grill the user about a finished task, logs first, and log workflow improvements that recur across retrospectives. Use after a task is marked done, when the user accepts the offered retro or asks for one. ([ghulando/halvard](https://github.com/ghulando/halvard/tree/HEAD/skills/halvard-retro) · ★4)
- **halvard-review**: Finish every task with a passing independent read-only review; fix once and re-review only after a blocked review. Use after project checks pass, including small fixes. ([ghulando/halvard](https://github.com/ghulando/halvard/tree/HEAD/skills/halvard-review) · ★4)
